Convert values to strings (HWPX text nodes are strings).\n    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        if v is None:\n            mapping[str(k)] = \"\"\n        elif isinstance(v, (int, float)):\n            mapping[str(k)] = str(v)\n        else:\n            mapping[str(k)] = str(v)\n\n    mapping = normalize_budget_fields(mapping)\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef normalize_budget_fields(mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Normalize currency strings for budget/cost keys.\n\n    Requirement: remove commas from numeric part while keeping leading currency symbol.\n    Example: ₩1,000,000 -> ₩1000000\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def looks_like_currency_amount(s: str) -> bool:\n        # Any digits with comma separators, optionally preceded by a currency symbol.\n        return bool(re.search(r\"\\d{1,3}(,\\d{3})+\", s))\n\n    def normalize_currency_commas(s: str) -> str:\n        # Keep any leading non-digit prefix (currency symbol, label), remove commas in digit chunks.\n        # E.g. '₩450,000,000' -> '₩450000000'\n        m = re.match(r\"^(\\D*)([\\d,]+)(.*)$\", s)\n        if not m:\n            return s\n        prefix, num, suffix = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)\n        return f\"{prefix}{num.replace(',', '')}{suffix}\"\n\n    budgetish_key_re = re.compile(r\"(예산|비용|경비|budget|cost)\", re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    out: Dict[str, str] = dict(mapping)\n    for k, v in mapping.items():\n        if not isinstance(v, str):\n            continue\n        if not budgetish_key_re.search(k):\n            continue\n        if looks_like_currency_amount(v):\n            out[k] = normalize_currency_commas(v)\n    return out\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key in mapping:\n            changed = True\n            return mapping[key]\n        # Unknown placeholder: leave as-is (but verification later will fail if any remain).\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\ndef extract_year_months(text: str) -> List[Tuple[int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"Extract year-month pairs in order of appearance.\n\n    Supports patterns like:\n    - 2026-08\n    - 2026.08\n    - 2026-08-01 (month is still captured)\n    - 2026.08.01\n    \"\"\"\n    pairs: List[Tuple[int, int]] = []\n    for m in re.finditer(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text):\n        y = int(m.group(1))\n        mo = int(m.group(2))\n        if 1 <= mo <= 12:\n            pairs.append((y, mo))\n    return pairs\n\n\ndef month_span_inclusive(start: Tuple[int, int], end: Tuple[int, int]) -> int:\n    sy, sm = start\n    ey, em = end\n    return (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm) + 1\n\n\ndef append_phase_month_span(text: str) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Append '(N개월)' to phase lines for 1단계/2단계/3단계.\n\n    We detect phase lines by presence of '1단계', '2단계', '3단계'.\n    Month span is derived from date ranges present in the line.\n    \"\"\"\n    if \"개월\" in text:\n        return text, False\n\n    if not any(tok in text for tok in (\"1단계\", \"2단계\", \"3단계\")):\n        return text, False\n\n    pairs = extract_year_months(text)\n    if len(pairs) >= 2:\n        months = month_span_inclusive(pairs[0], pairs[1])\n    elif len(pairs) == 1:\n        months = 1\n    else:\n        return text, False\n\n    new_text = text.rstrip() + f\" ({months}개월)\"\n    return new_text, True\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\") and \"}\" in tag:\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef remove_lineseg_elements(elem: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove descendant elements whose tag localname contains 'lineseg' (case-insensitive).\"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n\n    def recurse(parent: ET.Element):\n        nonlocal removed\n        # Iterate over a static list since we may modify children.\n        for child in list(parent):\n            lname = localname(child.tag).lower()\n            if \"lineseg\" in lname:\n                parent.remove(child)\n                removed += 1\n                continue\n            recurse(child)\n\n    recurse(elem)\n    return removed\n\n\ndef paragraph_text_nodes(p: ET.Element) -> List[ET.Element]:\n    # All hp:t descendants within the paragraph.\n    return list(p.findall(\".//hp:t\", NS))\n\n\ndef paragraph_full_text(ts: List[ET.Element]) -> str:\n    return \"\".join((t.text or \"\") for t in ts)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ChangeRecord:\n    file_path: str\n    paragraphs_changed: int\n    lineseg_removed: int\n\n\ndef process_section_xml(xml_path: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> ChangeRecord:\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    paragraphs_changed = 0\n    lineseg_removed_total = 0\n\n    for p in root.findall(\".//hp:p\", NS):\n        ts = paragraph_text_nodes(p)\n        if not ts:\n            continue\n\n        changed_here = False\n\n        # Pass 1: replace placeholders per text node.\n        for t in ts:\n            old = t.text or \"\"\n            new, ch = replace_placeholders_in_text(old, mapping)\n            if ch and new != old:\n                t.text = new\n                changed_here = True\n\n        # Pass 2: if placeholders still remain across boundaries, collapse.\n        full = paragraph_full_text(ts)\n        if \"{{\" in full and \"}}\" in full:\n            replaced, ch = replace_placeholders_in_text(full, mapping)\n            if ch and replaced != full:\n                ts[0].text = replaced\n                for t in ts[1:]:\n                    t.text = \"\"\n                changed_here = True\n            full = replaced\n\n        # Phase month-span append (operates on full paragraph text).\n        # Only attempt if we have at least one text node.\n        full_now = paragraph_full_text(ts)\n        appended, ch = append_phase_month_span(full_now)\n        if ch and appended != full_now:\n            ts[0].text = appended\n            for t in ts[1:]:\n                t.text = \"\"\n            changed_here = True\n\n        if changed_here:\n            paragraphs_changed += 1\n            # Remove layout cache elements from modified paragraphs.\n            lineseg_removed_total += remove_lineseg_elements(p)\n\n    out_bytes = ET.tostring(root, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n    if b\"{{\" in out_bytes or b\"}}\" in out_bytes:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Placeholders remain after processing: {xml_path}\")\n\n    with open(xml_path, \"wb\") as f:\n        f.write(out_bytes)\n\n    return ChangeRecord(\n        file_path=xml_path,\n        paragraphs_changed=paragraphs_changed,\n        lineseg_removed=lineseg_removed_total,\n    )\n\n\ndef extract_template() -> List[zipfile.ZipInfo]:\n    # Return original zip infos (for ordering/compression matching).\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_HWPX, \"r\") as z:\n        infos = z.infolist()\n        z.extractall(EXTRACT_DIR)\n        return infos\n\n\ndef repack_hwpx(original_infos: List[zipfile.ZipInfo]) -> None:\n    # Map original compression settings by filename.\n    comp_by_name = {zi.filename: zi.compress_type for zi in original_infos}\n    orig_order = [zi.filename for zi in original_infos]\n\n    # Gather extracted files.\n    extracted_files: List[str] = []\n    for root, _, files in os.walk(EXTRACT_DIR):\n        for fn in files:\n            extracted_files.append(os.path.join(root, fn))\n\n    # Build arcname mapping.\n    arc_by_path: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for fp in extracted_files:\n        arcname = os.path.relpath(fp, EXTRACT_DIR).replace(os.sep, \"/\")\n        arc_by_path[fp] = arcname\n\n    # Order files: follow original order first, then any extras.\n    ordered_arcnames: List[str] = []\n    existing_arcnames = set(arc_by_path.values())\n    for name in orig_order:\n        if name in existing_arcnames:\n            ordered_arcnames.append(name)\n    for name in sorted(existing_arcnames):\n        if name not in ordered_arcnames:\n            ordered_arcnames.append(name)\n\n    # Reverse mapping arcname -> filesystem path\n    path_by_arc = {arc: fp for fp, arc in arc_by_path.items()}\n\n    # Create zip.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_HWPX, \"w\") as out_z:\n        # If mimetype exists, store it first and uncompressed.\n        if \"mimetype\" in path_by_arc:\n            fp = path_by_arc[\"mimetype\"]\n            zi = zipfile.ZipInfo(\"mimetype\")\n            zi.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_STORED\n            with open(fp, \"rb\") as f:\n                out_z.writestr(zi, f.read())\n\n        for arcname in ordered_arcnames:\n            if arcname == \"mimetype\":\n                continue\n            fp = path_by_arc[arcname]\n            compress_type = comp_by_name.get(arcname, zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED)\n\n            # Use writestr with ZipInfo to set compression type explicitly.\n            zi = zipfile.ZipInfo(arcname)\n            zi.compress_type = compress_type\n            # Preserve a reasonable file mode (readable), but don't rely on original external_attr.\n            zi.external_attr = (0o644 & 0xFFFF) << 16\n            with open(fp, \"rb\") as f:\n                out_z.writestr(zi, f.read())\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    mapping = load_json_mapping(INPUT_JSON)\n\n    # We compute from the actual text we wrote.\n    # This is only a check; it does not modify anything.\n    expected = {\"1단계\": 3, \"2단계\": 3, \"3단계\": 1}\n    found: Dict[str, int] = {}\n    for fp in sorted(xml_files):\n        with open(fp, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            content = f.read()\n        for phase, exp in expected.items():\n            if phase in content:\n                m = re.search(re.escape(phase) + r\"[^\\n]*\\((\\d+)개월\\)\", content)\n                if m:\n                    found[phase] = int(m.group(1))\n    missing = [k for k in expected if k not in found]\n    if missing:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Missing month-span annotations for phases: {missing}\")\n    mismatched = {k: (found[k], expected[k]) for k in expected if found.get(k) != expected[k]}\n    if mismatched:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"Month-span annotations do not match benchmark: {mismatched}\")\n\n    # Repack.\n    repack_hwpx(original_infos)\n\n    # Final check: ensure output is a readable zip.\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_HWPX, \"r\") as z:\n        bad = z.testzip()\n        if bad is not None:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Output zip failed integrity check at member: {bad}\")\n\n    # Any digits with comma separators, optionally preceded by a currency symbol.\n        return bool(re.search(r\"\\d{1,3}(,\\d{3})+\", s))\n\n    def normalize_currency_commas(s: str) -> str:\n        # HWPX\n- Replaces {{placeholders}} in section XML files with JSON values\n- Normalizes budget fields (remove commas in numeric portion while keeping currency symbol)\n- Appends month-span '(N개월)' to phase lines (1단계/2단계/3단계) based on date ranges in text\n- Removes stale layout cache elements (linesegarray / any tag containing 'lineseg') from paragraphs whose text changed\n- Repackages the result as /root/project_proposal_ready.hwpx\n\nDesigned to preserve namespaces/prefixes using xml.etree.ElementTree.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nimport zipfile\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\n\n\nTEMPLATE_HWPX = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{\\s*([^{}\\s]+)\\s*\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef load_json_mapping(path: str) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    # Convert values to strings (HWPX text nodes are strings).\n    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in data.items():\n        if v is None:\n            mapping[str(k)] = \"\"\n        elif isinstance(v, (int, float)):\n            mapping[str(k)] = str(v)\n        else:\n            mapping[str(k)] = str(v)\n\n    mapping = normalize_budget_fields(mapping)\n    return mapping\n\n\ndef normalize_budget_fields(mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> Dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Normalize currency strings for budget/cost keys.\n\n    Requirement: remove commas from numeric part while keeping leading currency symbol.\n    Example: ₩1,000,000 -> ₩1000000\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def looks_like_currency_amount(s: str) -> bool:\n        # Any digits with comma separators, optionally preceded by a currency symbol.\n        return bool(re.search(r\"\\d{1,3}(,\\d{3})+\", s))\n\n    def normalize_currency_commas(s: str) -> str:\n        # Keep any leading non-digit prefix (currency symbol, label), remove commas in digit chunks.\n        # E.g. '₩450,000,000' -> '₩450000000'\n        m = re.match(r\"^(\\D*)([\\d,]+)(.*)$\", s)\n        if not m:\n            return s\n        prefix, num, suffix = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)\n        return f\"{prefix}{num.replace(',', '')}{suffix}\"\n\n    budgetish_key_re = re.compile(r\"(예산|비용|경비|budget|cost)\", re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    out: Dict[str, str] = dict(mapping)\n    for k, v in mapping.items():\n        if not isinstance(v, str):\n            continue\n        if not budgetish_key_re.search(k):\n            continue\n        if looks_like_currency_amount(v):\n            out[k] = normalize_currency_commas(v)\n    return out\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal changed\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key in mapping:\n            changed = True\n            return mapping[key]\n        # Unknown placeholder: leave as-is (but verification later will fail if any remain).\n        return m.group(0)\n\n    new_text = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(repl, text)\n    return new_text, changed\n\n\ndef extract_year_months(text: str) -> List[Tuple[int, int]]:\n    \"\"\"Extract year-month pairs in order of appearance.\n\n    Supports patterns like:\n    - 2026-08\n    - 2026.08\n    - 2026-08-01 (month is still captured)\n    - 2026.08.01\n    \"\"\"\n    pairs: List[Tuple[int, int]] = []\n    for m in re.finditer(r\"(\\d{4})[.\\-](\\d{2})\", text):\n        y = int(m.group(1))\n        mo = int(m.group(2))\n        if 1 <= mo <= 12:\n            pairs.append((y, mo))\n    return pairs\n\n\ndef month_span_inclusive(start: Tuple[int, int], end: Tuple[int, int]) -> int:\n    sy, sm = start\n    ey, em = end\n    return (ey - sy) * 12 + (em - sm) + 1\n\n\ndef append_phase_month_span(text: str) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    \"\"\"Append '(N개월)' to phase lines for 1단계/2단계/3단계.\n\n    We detect phase lines by presence of '1단계', '2단계', '3단계'.\n    Month span is derived from date ranges present in the line.\n    \"\"\"\n    if \"개월\" in text:\n        return text, False\n\n    if not any(tok in text for tok in (\"1단계\", \"2단계\", \"3단계\")):\n        return text, False\n\n    pairs = extract_year_months(text)\n    if len(pairs) >= 2:\n        months = month_span_inclusive(pairs[0], pairs[1])\n    elif len(pairs) == 1:\n        months = 1\n    else:\n        return text, False\n\n    new_text = text.rstrip() + f\" ({months}개월)\"\n    return new_text, True\n\n\ndef localname(tag: str) -> str:\n    if tag.startswith(\"{\") and \"}\" in tag:\n        return tag.split(\"}\", 1)[1]\n    return tag\n\n\ndef remove_lineseg_elements(elem: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove descendant elements whose tag localname contains 'lineseg' (case-insensitive).\"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n\n    def recurse(parent: ET.Element):\n        nonlocal removed\n        # Pass 1: replace placeholders per text node.\n        for t in ts:\n            old = t.text or \"\"\n            new, ch = replace_placeholders_in_text(old, mapping)\n            if ch and new != old:\n                t.text = new\n                changed_here = True\n\n        # Pass 2: if placeholders still remain across boundaries, collapse.\n        full = paragraph_full_text(ts)\n        if \"{{\" in full and \"}}\" in full:\n            replaced, ch = replace_placeholders_in_text(full, mapping)\n            if ch and replaced != full:\n                ts[0].text = replaced\n                for t in ts[1:]:\n                    t.text = \"\"\n                changed_here = True\n            full = replaced\n\n        #
